Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in deep theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 112 and the invariant I(A,B) = 358 mod 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_112(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 112. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^358(S, F) is trivial. QED.
